Obol/libbrlobol Backend Worklog
===============================

Purpose
-------

This file is the append/update log for the Obol/libbrlobol migration.  Keep
`backend_capabilities.txt` focused on current status and remaining work.  Use
this file for detailed completed-work notes, bridge retirements, test evidence,
and guard updates.


Update Rules
------------

* Keep entries structured and easy to append automatically.
* Add new entries under "Log Entries" using the template at the end of this
  file.
* Use UTC dates in `YYYY-MM-DD` form.
* Keep `id` stable, lowercase, and hyphenated.
* Keep `capability` as one of:
  `scene-source`, `feature-polygon`, `render-export`, `display-stream`,
  `qtcad-ownership`, `docs`, or `guard`.
* Prefer concise bullets over long prose.
* Put detailed file/test evidence in the entry where the work happened.
* Update `backend_capabilities.txt` only when current status or remaining work
  changes.


Current Baseline Snapshot
-------------------------

date: 2026-07-02
status: closeout
active_capability: scene-source

Capability 1 baseline:

* Obol/qtcad has staged scene, group, source, placement, display-state,
  revision, summary, and draw-sync coverage.
* GED public records and transaction paths increasingly use neutral summaries,
  source-state records, draw-state summaries, source snapshots, child
  iterators, and facade operations instead of direct BSG field access.
* The large Obol-primary GED database draw-scene behavior-family push has
  landed.  The split draw-scene shard set is green for database draw, erase,
  redraw, rename, reference-removal, path-state, path-listing, bounds,
  display/material, source-summary, and record-query coverage.
* The BSG-backed source adapter still exists as legacy/no-owned-scene
  compatibility and reference implementation code, but it should not be the
  active database scene/source owner for migrated behavior.
* `src/libged/bsg_ged_draw_private.h` still exposes transitional
  `rt_view_scene_ref` facades while live GED internals need neutral handles
  for retained scene tokens.
* `misc/CMake/brlobol_pivot_guard.cmake` is the authoritative staged-boundary
  guard for this migration phase and now covers the newly migrated
  Obol-primary path-state/listing/source-rename/ref-remove boundary.

Capability 2 baseline:

* Feature and polygon behavior still needs a full Obol/libbrlobol-backed store
  behind neutral `rt_view_*` and `ged_draw_view_*` APIs.
* `src/librt/view_legacy_bsg.c` and `src/libged/bsg_ged_draw_view.c` remain
  transition points for feature/polygon behavior.

Capability 3 baseline:

* Obol export action has staged object-record support with stable ordering,
  identity fields, filters, geometry details, and full-detail request metadata.
* GED view-record wrappers route through a neutral backend-adapter producer.
* The temporary BSG export producer remains in the BSG-backed source adapter
  pending Obol query hookup.

Capability 4 baseline:

* The permanent Obol/libbrlobol display draw stream still needs implementation
  sufficient to feed qtcad directly and temporarily validate libdm consumers.
* `src/libdm/view.c` still must lose direct BSG render API dependency before
  the libdm/libbsg dependency row can retire.

Capability 5 baseline:

* qtcad Obol sync/export/pick/snap/measure/window-host work is staged, but
  normal qtcad/qged visible rendering is not yet fully Obol/libbrlobol-owned.
* `qged` direct `dm_plugins` dependency must remain until qtcad/Obol owns the
  normal display backend path.


Log Entries
-----------

  id: scene-source-closeout-gate-audit
  capability: scene-source

    refresh.

      shard regex.

      `src/libged/bsg_ged_draw_records.c`,
      `src/libged/bsg_ged_draw_transactions.c`, `src/libged/draw_obol.cpp`,
      and `include/ged/draw.h`.

      fails immediately under CTest's headless `QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen`
      setup because the Qt offscreen plugin cannot create an OpenGL context.
  files:
    - `backend_capabilities.txt`
    - `backend_worklog.txt`
    - `doc/notes/obol_legacy_dependency_inventory.txt`
    - `obol_activework.txt`
  tests:
    - `ctest --test-dir build -R '^ged_test_ged_draw_scene_.*$' -j 8 --timeout 60 --output-on-failure`
    - `ctest --test-dir build -R '^test_qtcad_obol_draw_sync$' --timeout 180 --output-on-failure` (failed; Qt offscreen OpenGL context unsupported)

    - `ctest --test-dir build -R '^(brlobol_pivot_guard|ged_test_state_services|ged_test_ged_draw_scene_|ged_test_obol_draw_sync|test_qtcad_obol_controller|test_qtcad_obol_window_host|test_qtcad_obol_draw_sync|test_qtcad_obol_edit_preview|test_qtcad_obol_faceplate_sync|test_qtcad_obol_selection_sync|test_qtcad_obol_pick|test_qtcad_obol_export|test_qtcad_obol_snap|test_qtcad_obol_measure|test_qtcad_obol_overlay_sync)$' -j 8 --timeout 180 --output-on-failure` (partial; draw-scene prefix regex was too strict, qtcad GL-dependent tests failed, other listed tests passed)
  notes:
    - Superseded by `qtcad-obol-swrast-closeout-gate`: the intentional
      Qt+OSMesa qtcad test path now exists and passes.  System OpenGL remains
      a follow-up spot check.

entry:
  date: 2026-06-26
  id: scene-source-all-paths-subpath-erase-source-adapter
  capability: scene-source
  summary: Moved all-paths DB-subpath group erase loops into the source
    adapter.
  changed:
    - Added private `ged_draw_scene_ref_erase_groups_by_db_subpath` in the
      current BSG-backed source adapter.
    - Unscoped and scoped all-paths erase now ask the source adapter to erase
      matching retained groups under a resolved DB subpath instead of tree code
      owning retained group traversal and restart loops.
    - Source adapter now owns restartable group-child snapshotting, optional
      scope filtering, group path to DB-full-path conversion, full-path subset
      matching, ancestor-depth calculation, retained group free, and nested
      DB-subpath erase dispatch for this helper.
    - Tree all-paths erase keeps only DB path conversion, draw-revision
      fallback accounting, and neutral shape-record release fallback.
    - Removed the now-unused tree-local `_sg_group_matches_erase_scope`
      helper; scoped matching now happens inside the source adapter.
    - Pivot guard now requires the all-paths DB-subpath erase facade, rejects
      lower nested DB-subpath dispatch from returning to tree, and rejects the
      retired tree-local `_sg_group_matches_erase_scope` helper.
  files:

      source-default behavior for callers that omit the state.

      published, matching the rest of the source-owned realized-shape
      placement path.
    - Updated the libged Obol bridge to translate neutral
      `ged_draw_scene_display_summary` state into the libbrlobol auxiliary
      display state before publishing source-adapter auxiliary geometry.
    - Extended direct libbrlobol and GED Obol sync tests to verify auxiliary
      display state and placement inheritance.
    - Updated the pivot guard and Capability 1 notes for auxiliary
      display/placement publication.

entry:
  date: YYYY-MM-DD
  id: short-stable-id
  capability: scene-source|feature-polygon|render-export|display-stream|qtcad-ownership|docs|guard
  summary: One concise sentence.
  changed:
    - Concrete completed change.
  files:
    - `path/to/file`
  tests:
    - command or `not run; reason`
  notes:
    - Optional context, guard implication, or follow-up.
